Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No general monotonic benefit assumption is supported; appropriate scope and human readiness must be established instead.

**Observable postcondition.** Autonomy choices are justified by the task rather than a universal score or prestige claim.

**Important criticisms.** **Source grounded objection.** Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.

**Criticism ids.** EAA-C003; EAA-C007

**Source ids.** EAA-S015; EAA-S020; EAA-S043; EAA-S036

**Analytical adversarial test.** Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Known failure modes.** Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.; Introduced cost or harm: Overreach, burdens on others and prolonged ineffective action.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.

**Source ids.** EAA-S015; EAA-S020; EAA-S043; EAA-S036

**Is general refutation.** True

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** What specific gain justifies this added autonomy, and which newly introduced failure must be controlled?; This does not imply manual control always wins; oversight can be costly or infeasible.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Replace monotonic autonomy with conditional capability, authority and architecture selection.

**Condition.** Claims that less oversight or greater persistence is inherently a maturity gain.

**Limit.** Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True

## EAA-058 — A complete central world model is universally necessary

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-058

**Native name.** A complete central world model is universally necessary

**Selected status.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Bearer and scope.** The claim that every adequate autonomous system requires a complete central world model.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Universal architectural necessity is rejected; some tasks still require rich models.

**Applicability guard.** Central completeness is proposed as a prerequisite for agency or successful action.

**Material premises.** Adequacy is judged by a task, not by representation volume.

**Evaluation rule.** A task-sufficient reactive, distributed or compiled policy is a counterexample to universal necessity.

**Constructed positive case.** A model-based planner is retained where anticipation demonstrably helps.

**Constructed failure witness.** A small feedback controller accomplishes its bounded task without representing every object and relation in the environment.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Use the minimal sufficient state and add modelling when a missing distinction causes failure.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED. Brooks’ alternative and compiled controller observations undermine universality; neither licenses deleting representations needed for partially observed tasks.

## EAA-059 — Representation-free reaction is universally sufficient

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-059

**Native name.** Representation-free reaction is universally sufficient

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/58

**Original status.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Selected status.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Bearer and scope.** The claim that representation-free reaction is sufficient for every autonomous task.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Universal sufficiency is rejected; finite reactive success remains legitimate.

**Applicability guard.** A memoryless or representation-free policy is prescribed without regard to task history or hidden state.

**Material premises.** The task may require distinctions absent from the current observation.

**Evaluation rule.** Exhibit two histories with identical present observation but incompatible required actions; the common reactive response cannot satisfy both.

**Constructed positive case.** A reactive obstacle-avoidance controller works in its declared envelope.

**Constructed failure witness.** A robot must choose a door based on a previously observed signal no longer visible; identical current inputs require different actions.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Add a single bit of memory when sufficient, rather than jumping to a complete symbolic model.

## EAA-060 — Proxy maximisation is sufficient objective fulfilment

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-060

**Native name.** Proxy maximisation is sufficient objective fulfilment

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/59

**Original status.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Selected status.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Bearer and scope.** The universal implication from maximizing a measured proxy to fulfilling the intended objective.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Across all environments and protected requirements; rejected by a valid proxy-goal divergence.

**Applicability guard.** A reward or score is treated as a sufficient definition of success without testing its correspondence.

**Material premises.** The intended objective can differ from the proxy under at least one admissible condition.

**Evaluation rule.** Reject the implication when high proxy value coexists with a missed goal or violated protected means.

**Constructed positive case.** A carefully scoped exact metric can correctly identify success within its validated domain.

**Constructed failure witness.** An agent reaches the scored terminal state by skipping a required authorization step.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Retain the metric as partial evidence and add a targeted check for the material omitted obligation.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED at universal scope. Goal-misgeneralization experiments and τ-bench limitations provide substantive pressure; no claim that all metrics inevitably fail.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S025; EAA-S033

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

**Source id.** EAA-S033

**Date.** 2024-06

## EAA-061 — Mandatory continuous reflection

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-061

**Native name.** Mandatory continuous reflection

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/60

**Original status.** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM

**Selected status.** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM

**Bearer and scope.** The prescription that every agent must continuously reflect on its behavior.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Universal and continuous requirement is superseded by conditional reflection and metareasoning.

**Applicability guard.** Reflection is proposed regardless of task complexity, feedback quality or cost.

**Material premises.** Useful revision requires informative feedback and spare resources.

**Evaluation rule.** Compare reflection’s marginal benefit with direct action, fixed correction and bounded retry; omit it when no benefit is supported.

**Constructed positive case.** Reflection helps diagnose a repeatedly failing programming attempt with reliable tests.

**Constructed failure witness.** An already correct trivial task is made slower or worse by repeated self-critique.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** EAA-054’s guarded reflection and EAA-028’s computation budget replace the universal rule.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM as a logical narrowing, not a claim of a historically established doctrine’s demise. Reflexion’s adverse cases and resource analysis support the narrower form.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S031; EAA-S035; EAA-S014

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

**Original form.** Analytical extrapolation from reflective methods to a mandatory always-on ritual.

**Historical origin.** **Original or inspected anchors.** **Source id.** EAA-S031

**Date.** 2023-10-10

**Work.** Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning

**Version.** arXiv:2303.11366v4

**Attribution limit.** These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Current status.** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM

**Mechanism.** The superseded form reflects after every event or indefinitely, independent of feedback quality and marginal value.

**Trigger or context.** Adding always-on self-critique or repeated self-improvement prompts.

**Non trigger or cheap path.** Use a direct correction, fixed policy or bounded externally evaluated reflection only when it is expected to help.

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** Useful feedback, a feasible next attempt and a task-specific budget are prerequisites for the retained replacement.

**Property ids.** None recorded.

**Relation semantics.** A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Actor and authority boundary.** **Actor or consumer.** Revision designer and deliberation scheduler

**Boundary.** The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Useful feedback, a feasible next attempt and a task-specific budget are prerequisites for the retained replacement.

**Observable postcondition.** Reflection is invoked conditionally and stopped when it fails to produce useful evidence or improvement.

**Important criticisms.** **Source grounded objection.** Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.

**Criticism ids.** EAA-C009

**Source ids.** EAA-S031; EAA-S035; EAA-S014

**Analytical adversarial test.** Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Known failure modes.** Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.; Introduced cost or harm: Tokens, latency, local minima and contaminated memory.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.

**Source ids.** EAA-S031; EAA-S035; EAA-S014

**Is general refutation.** False

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** What would be lost by omitting this reflection, and what evidence justifies its next iteration?; A larger budget may help some tasks but does not prove every additional reflective step is worthwhile.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Superseded by EAA-054 external-feedback-anchored reflection and EAA-028 value-sensitive deliberation.

**Condition.** Adding always-on self-critique or repeated self-improvement prompts.

**Limit.** Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True

## EAA-062 — General assurance for open-ended self-revision

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-062

**Native name.** General assurance for open-ended self-revision

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/61

**Original status.** UNRESOLVED

**Selected status.** UNRESOLVED

**Bearer and scope.** A general assurance claim for agents that can revise their own policies, representations, goals or evaluation criteria in open-ended environments.

**Quantifier and horizon.** The proposed scope exceeds a fixed finite test suite or the cited narrow formal models.

**Applicability guard.** Assurance is asserted across unconstrained self-revision and environmental change.

**Material premises.** A proposed result would need explicit allowed modifications, preserved criterion, correspondence and compositional reasoning over future changes.

**Evaluation rule.** Keep the general claim unresolved unless a result supplies those conditions; failure to find it is not a proof of impossibility.

**Constructed positive case.** A restricted self-modification theorem or a bounded regression-tested update can be supported at its narrower scope.

**Constructed failure witness.** A policy preserves a mistaken initial utility, or changes its evaluator so that its own failures disappear from the metric.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Constrain the update class, keep a fixed external criterion or decline self-modification when that is adequate.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep UNRESOLVED after inspecting Everitt’s theorem, EWC and distributional goal failures. A discriminating next result would establish a nontrivial modification class with preserved task-relevant guarantees and a credible implementation/environment relation, or provide a scoped impossibility theorem.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S053; EAA-S026; EAA-S025; EAA-S041

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION

**Date.** 2017-01-25

**Work.** Overcoming catastrophic forgetting in neural networks

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** An acceptable revision criterion, trustworthy evaluation and implementation/environment assumptions; their general sufficiency remains unresolved.

**Property ids.** None recorded.

## EAA-063 — Belief/intention prompts without operational semantics

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-063

**Selected status.** CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Bearer and scope.** An attribution of BDI or intentional operational semantics based only on prompt labels.

**Quantifier and horizon.** For the semantic attribution, not for every possible empirical effect of wording.

**Applicability guard.** Words such as belief or intention are used to claim a corresponding architecture.

**Material premises.** Operational state and transition rules are absent or not established.

**Evaluation rule.** Do not infer BDI semantics from the label alone; investigate prompt utility separately with an appropriate comparison.

**Constructed positive case.** A prompt can use intention as a useful informal planning cue while making no claim of BDI equivalence.

**Constructed failure witness.** Renaming a scratchpad beliefs is treated as implementing a belief-revision logic.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Use plain task descriptions or implement explicit semantics only when they serve the task.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY for the unsupported semantic inference. Shoham and Rao–Georgeff make the operational demand clear; this is not a blanket empirical claim that mentalistic prompts never help.

**Material premises.** Operational state and transition rules are absent or not established.

**Evaluation rule.** Do not infer BDI semantics from the label alone; investigate prompt utility separately with an appropriate comparison.

**Constructed positive case.** A prompt can use intention as a useful informal planning cue while making no claim of BDI equivalence.

**Constructed failure witness.** Renaming a scratchpad beliefs is treated as implementing a belief-revision logic.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Use plain task descriptions or implement explicit semantics only when they serve the task.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY for the unsupported semantic inference. Shoham and Rao–Georgeff make the operational demand clear; this is not a blanket empirical claim that mentalistic prompts never help.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S010; EAA-S009; EAA-S030

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

## EAA-064 — Leaderboard success as deployment guarantee

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-064

**Native name.** Leaderboard success as deployment guarantee

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/63

**Original status.** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Selected status.** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Bearer and scope.** The inference from a benchmark or leaderboard result to a deployment guarantee.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Universal transfer beyond the measured tasks, versions, oracles and operating conditions is rejected.

**Applicability guard.** A score is used to assert reliable behavior in a broader real-world setting.

**Material premises.** Task distribution, evaluation reliability, tool conditions and consequences would need correspondence evidence.

**Evaluation rule.** State the measured result at its actual scope and test material deployment differences before extending it.

**Constructed positive case.** A benchmark score is used to screen candidates for a further scoped evaluation.

**Constructed failure witness.** A high average score is used to promise repeated policy-compliant operation under unseen user behavior and tool failures.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** A narrow task-specific tool can remain preferable even when it has no general leaderboard entry.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Keep NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY. WebArena, τ-bench, cost/reproducibility criticism and METR’s horizon definition jointly defeat the guarantee inference; they do not make benchmarks useless. New reliability work further separates dimensions concealed by aggregate success.

## EAA-066 — Software–physical translation needs new obligations

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-066

**Native name.** Software–physical translation needs new obligations

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/65

**Original status.** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Selected status.** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Bearer and scope.** A mechanism or performance claim transferred between software-only and embodied action environments.

**Quantifier and horizon.** For the concrete sensors, actuators, timing and physical effects of the proposed transfer.

**Applicability guard.** Physical dynamics or irreversible effects can invalidate software-level assumptions.

**Material premises.** The added correspondence obligations are specified and supported at the relevant operating envelope.

**Evaluation rule.** Evaluate sensing error, actuation feasibility, timing, resource and stopping behavior before extending a software result to physical outcomes.

**Constructed positive case.** A tool-planning pattern is retained but grounded in measured robot skills and separately tested recovery behavior.

**Constructed failure witness.** A successful simulated command sequence is presented as evidence of safe hardware execution despite unmodeled actuator delays.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** A purely informational software task does not acquire physical control requirements merely because the same architecture name is used.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Retain strongly. Weld’s software effectors, DS1, SayCan and the recent robot-crew case expose different translation boundaries. Twenty simulations and two hardware demonstrations cannot establish general physical reliability.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S045; EAA-S022; EAA-S049; EAA-S039

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

## EAA-067 — Coherent action basis across asynchronous state, goal and authority changes

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-067

**Native name.** Coherent action basis across asynchronous state, goal and authority changes

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/66

**Original status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Selected status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Bearer and scope.** The joint action basis of an asynchronous controller whose state, goal or authority information can change across components.

**Quantifier and horizon.** At each consequential dispatch and through the relevant handoff interval; consistency is relative to material assumptions, not global simultaneity.

**Applicability guard.** Independent updates can combine individually valid but mutually incompatible justifications for one action.

**Material premises.** The material state, goal and authority versions or equivalent atomicity conditions can be related; BDI, hybrid layers and revocation are conditional contributors, not universally necessary modules.

**Evaluation rule.** Ensure the dispatched action’s material justifications are jointly compatible or reject/recompute it. If EAA-022 already includes every justification atomically, this criterion is subsumed.

**Constructed positive case.** A plan for destination A is rejected when a concurrent goal change selects B, even though the route and permissions each passed separate checks.

**Constructed failure witness.** A fresh location, an old goal and a new narrow permission are combined into an action that no coherent accepted task ever justified.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** An atomic serial controller can satisfy the criterion implicitly; no version registry or separate coherence owner is intrinsically required.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Retain as a useful derived obligation, not a new empirically validated independent guarantee. Parent-removal tests show that EAA-013, 019 and 040 are unnecessary in fixed-goal, single-controller, immutable-authority cases. Record conditional parent roles and the EAA-022 subsumption boundary.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S022; EAA-S016; EAA-S019; EAA-S039

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION; ANALYTICAL_REDECOMPOSITION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

**Original form.** No single native original form: researcher-composed invariant from state freshness, commitments, scope and asynchronous execution.

**Historical origin.** **Original or inspected anchors.** **Source id.** EAA-S022

**Date.** 1999

**Work.** Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment

**Version.** ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report

**Source id.** EAA-S016

**Date.** 1987

**Work.** An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains

**Version.** AAAI 1987 pp.202–206

**Source id.** EAA-S019

**Date.** 1999

**Work.** Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces

**Version.** CHI 1999; author manuscript

**Source id.** EAA-S039

**Date.** 2026-08-23

**Work.** Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs

**Version.** arXiv:2608.22657v1

**Attribution limit.** These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Current status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Mechanism.** Ensure a consequential action is evaluated against a mutually compatible current-enough basis of relevant state, adopted goal and delegated scope; invalidate or recheck that basis after material concurrent changes.

**Trigger or context.** Asynchronous observations, planners, human updates and in-flight actions share mutable decision context.

**Non trigger or cheap path.** A single atomic constrained operation or serial bounded controller may already ensure coherence; no universal transaction protocol is prescribed.

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** Identifiable dependencies between state, goal, authority and action, plus an enforceable refresh/invalidation point.

**Property ids.** EAA-004; EAA-006; EAA-010; EAA-013; EAA-019; EAA-022; EAA-040

**Relation semantics.** CONDITIONALLY_DRAWS_ON: the listed properties contribute when their state, goal, authority, intention or arbitration concern is material. They are not all necessary parents in every case and do not require dedicated modules. An atomic EAA-022 contract covering all material justifications can subsume the coherence criterion.

**Actor and authority boundary.** **Actor or consumer.** Composition designer and executor

**Boundary.** The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identifiable dependencies between state, goal, authority and action, plus an enforceable refresh/invalidation point.

**Observable postcondition.** An action cannot be justified by combining individually valid facts that never jointly governed the same decision.

**Important criticisms.** **Source grounded objection.** This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.

**Criticism ids.** EAA-C005

**Source ids.** EAA-S022; EAA-S039

**Analytical adversarial test.** Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Known failure modes.** This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.; Introduced cost or harm: Coordination, serialisation, cancelled work and difficulty observing distributed effects.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.

**Source ids.** EAA-S022; EAA-S039

**Is general refutation.** False

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** Could this action combine evidence, intent and permission from incompatible moments?; Only relevant dependencies need coherence; atomicity, acceptable staleness and concurrent change cannot be assumed from a diagram.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Retain a composition obligation with alternative implementations and explicit timing/observation limits.

**Condition.** Asynchronous observations, planners, human updates and in-flight actions share mutable decision context.

**Limit.** This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True

## EAA-068 — Failure-attributed revision rather than undirected self-change

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-068

**Native name.** Failure-attributed revision rather than undirected self-change

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/67

**Original status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Selected status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Bearer and scope.** A repair or persistent revision chosen after a failure in an adaptive agent system.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Across diagnosis, change and reevaluation, limited by the available evidence and task budget.

**Applicability guard.** More than one plausible cause would justify materially different changes, especially when a persistent change is proposed.

**Material premises.** Failure evidence distinguishes at least some hypotheses; certainty about every root cause is not required before a safe local correction.

**Evaluation rule.** Match the change to a supported failure hypothesis and evaluate the affected outcome; preserve uncertainty and avoid changing unrelated mechanisms solely to produce activity.

**Constructed positive case.** An explicit actuator-unavailable error causes a capability substitution, while a faulty success oracle causes an evaluation repair.

**Constructed failure witness.** A missed goal caused by a stale observation is answered by rewriting the reward function, leaving the causal fault intact.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** A known deterministic local error can be corrected directly without a general diagnosis platform, proxy analysis or runtime assurance monitor.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Retain as a composition-derived decision rule. EAA-005, 023, 024, 034, 045 and 050 contribute under different failure hypotheses; removing proxy analysis or a general monitor need not break a direct actuator repair. This is not a universal causal-identification algorithm or evidence of improved field outcomes.

**Mechanism.** Use discriminating observations to separate action failure, observation error, model mismatch, bad evaluation, goal ambiguity and inadequate capability; change only an authorised relevant mechanism and test the revised claim.

**Trigger or context.** Repeated failure or a proposed persistent change after an adverse outcome.

**Non trigger or cheap path.** Apply a known local correction when the cause is established; otherwise preserve uncertainty and avoid broad self-modification.

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** Outcome evidence, candidate failure explanations, a bounded change surface and a meaningful subsequent test.

**Property ids.** EAA-005; EAA-023; EAA-024; EAA-034; EAA-045; EAA-050

**Relation semantics.** CONDITIONALLY_DRAWS_ON: use the evidence and tests relevant to the failure hypothesis. A direct known actuator error can justify a local correction without proxy analysis or a general runtime monitor. No complete causal diagnosis or separate module for every listed property is required.

**Actor and authority boundary.** **Actor or consumer.** Executor, evaluator and authorised revision process

**Boundary.** The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Outcome evidence, candidate failure explanations, a bounded change surface and a meaningful subsequent test.

**Known failure modes.** Failure causes can be indistinguishable from available observations; causal diagnosis may be more expensive than safe handback.; Introduced cost or harm: Diagnosis cost, delayed recovery and false causal confidence.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** Failure causes can be indistinguishable from available observations; causal diagnosis may be more expensive than safe handback.

**Source ids.** EAA-S031; EAA-S025

**Is general refutation.** False

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** Which component actually failed, and what observation would show that the proposed change targets the wrong cause?; The composed diagnostic discipline is analytical and not established as one empirically validated architecture.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Retain testable failure attribution and bounded revision, not a requirement to diagnose every failure completely before any safe response.

**Condition.** Repeated failure or a proposed persistent change after an adverse outcome.

**Limit.** Failure causes can be indistinguishable from available observations; causal diagnosis may be more expensive than safe handback.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True

<a id="eaa-069"></a>

## EAA-069 — Goal- and scope-preserving plan repair

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-069

**Native name.** Goal- and scope-preserving plan repair

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/68

**Original status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Selected status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Bearer and scope.** A repaired plan together with the still-authorized goal and protected scope.

**Quantifier and horizon.** Across the change from the failed plan to the replacement and its consequential actions.

**Applicability guard.** A local repair could obtain nominal success by changing the goal or exceeding delegation.

**Material premises.** The goal, protected constraints and effective authority are sufficiently explicit; legitimate task revision follows a separate authorized decision.

**Evaluation rule.** Evaluate the replacement against the same surviving goal and scope, or explicitly record an authorized revision before claiming success.

**Constructed positive case.** A failed train route is replaced by another permitted route to the same destination within the agreed budget.

**Constructed failure witness.** To finish a purchase, the agent changes the destination, spends beyond the limit or bypasses required consent.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** A fixed repair library whose alternatives already preserve the contract can satisfy this implicitly; a failed task can end honestly without repair.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Retain as a derived conjunction of EAA-004, EAA-010 and EAA-024, with EAA-025 and EAA-067 conditionally relevant. Populate the previously empty explicit parent list. The criterion adds a useful joint check, but does not establish a wholly independent property beyond scope-aware bounded repair.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S006; EAA-S019; EAA-S039; EAA-S033

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION; ANALYTICAL_REDECOMPOSITION

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

**Original form.** No single comprehensive native form: practical goal continuity and delegated scope combined with a recent request-preserving action gate.

**Historical origin.** **Original or inspected anchors.** **Source id.** EAA-S006

**Date.** 1988

**Work.** Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning

**Version.** Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript

**Source id.** EAA-S019

**Date.** 1999

**Work.** Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces

**Version.** CHI 1999; author manuscript

**Source id.** EAA-S039

**Date.** 2026-08-23

**Work.** Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs

**Version.** arXiv:2608.22657v1

**Attribution limit.** These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Current status.** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Mechanism.** Allow alternatives in means only within the current task and authority; treat changes to the intended goal, named entities or protected constraints as a separate decision requiring appropriate authorisation.

**Trigger or context.** Failed plans, unavailable destinations/tools or a generated substitute that would improve a success score.

**Non trigger or cheap path.** A pre-authorised equivalent alternative can be selected directly; a harmless change of means need not require a new conversation.

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** Identifiable invariant task conditions, permissible degrees of freedom and a route to negotiate genuine goal changes.

**Property ids.** EAA-004; EAA-010; EAA-024

**Relation semantics.** DERIVED_CONJUNCTION: bounded repair preserves the still-effective goal and delegation. Existing scope-aware repair contracts may already subsume this criterion; an authorized equivalent alternative needs no new approval or message.

**Actor and authority boundary.** **Actor or consumer.** Planner, executor and delegator

**Boundary.** The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identifiable invariant task conditions, permissible degrees of freedom and a route to negotiate genuine goal changes.

**Observable postcondition.** Repaired execution still pursues the authorised request, or the changed task is explicitly accepted rather than hidden as success.

**Important criticisms.** **Source grounded objection.** Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.

**Criticism ids.** EAA-C006

**Source ids.** EAA-S033; EAA-S039

**Analytical adversarial test.** Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Known failure modes.** Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.; Introduced cost or harm: Blocked useful substitutions and clarification latency.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.

**Source ids.** EAA-S033; EAA-S039

**Is general refutation.** False

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** Did this repair change only the means, or did it quietly change what was requested or permitted?; Goal equivalence and delegator intent need task-specific evidence; the invariant is broader analytical synthesis than one paper’s contract gate.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Retain explicit separation of plan revision from goal/authority revision, with user negotiation when task meaning changes.

**Condition.** Failed plans, unavailable destinations/tools or a generated substitute that would improve a success score.

**Limit.** Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True

<a id="eaa-070"></a>

## EAA-070 — Predecessor-criterion evaluation of self-modification

### Current scientific adjudication

**Property id.** EAA-070

**Native name.** Predecessor-criterion evaluation of self-modification

**Original locator.** EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/69

**Original status.** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Selected status.** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Bearer and scope.** A self-modifying decision-theoretic agent evaluated using its predecessor’s utility criterion.

**Quantifier and horizon.** The theorem’s stipulated future histories and initial realistic optimality, not arbitrary finite implementations or every open-ended revision.

**Applicability guard.** A modification can change policy or utility and its evaluation is made before the modification.

**Material premises.** Modification-independent belief and initial utility, the model’s anticipation/optimality conditions and the exact theorem statement are required for the formal conclusion. Ethical or practical acceptability of the initial criterion is a separate application obligation.

**Evaluation rule.** Evaluate the successor using the predecessor criterion under the formal model; separately assess whether that criterion should govern the real task and whether the implementation belongs to the model.

**Constructed positive case.** A modeled agent rejects a self-modification that would reduce value according to its original utility.

**Constructed failure witness.** A mistaken or harmful initial utility is faithfully preserved, or an approximate learner is assumed to inherit an ideal optimality result.

**Nontrigger and cheaper alternative.** Use restricted updates with an external regression criterion when the theorem’s premises are unavailable; ordinary state updates are not utility self-modification.

**Independent judgement and evidence ceiling.** Retain as ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE. Everitt et al.’s Theorem 12 supports a narrow formal result. Clarify the combined formal/practical dependency sentence: the native record already warns that a wrong criterion may be preserved, so its combined applicability guard is not evidence of a false theorem claim. The formal/application separation is documentation precision; no proof of generally beneficial or safe self-revision follows.

**Freshly inspected source ids.** EAA-S053; EAA-S025; EAA-S041

**Change basis.** NEW_STANDARD_OBLIGATION; DOCUMENTATION_REPAIR

**Analysis origin.** New author analysis; examples are constructed unless an identified empirical case is explicitly cited. Native historical source and review ceilings remain separate.

**Original form.** A formal realistic value function evaluates anticipated policy changes under the predecessor utility rather than a successor’s rewritten utility.

**Historical origin.** **Original or inspected anchors.** **Source id.** EAA-S053

**Date.** 2016

**Work.** Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents

**Version.** AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1

**Attribution limit.** These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Current status.** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Mechanism.** In the specified formal model, evaluate anticipated post-change policy consequences using the predecessor utility and modification-independent beliefs, rather than letting a successor utility certify itself.

**Trigger or context.** Explicit policy or utility self-modification under a model capable of representing its future effects.

**Non trigger or cheap path.** Use a fixed mechanism or externally reviewed bounded update when broad self-modification is unnecessary or its assumptions cannot be established.

**Dependencies or preconditions.** **Conditions.** Formal result: the specified model’s optimality/anticipation and modification-independence conditions, including evaluation by the predecessor utility. Practical application additionally requires a justified initial criterion and an adequate implementation/environment relation; those are not premises needed for the mathematical theorem to be true.

**Property ids.** None recorded.

**Relation semantics.** A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Actor and authority boundary.** **Actor or consumer.** Formal agent designer and authorised revision reviewer

**Boundary.** The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. The model’s optimality/anticipation and modification-independence conditions, plus a justified initial criterion.

**Observable postcondition.** The theorem’s predecessor-utility value is preserved on reachable histories under its assumptions; no broader safety or corrigibility result is asserted.

**Important criticisms.** **Source grounded objection.** Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.

**Criticism ids.** EAA-C008; EAA-C013

**Source ids.** EAA-S053; EAA-S025; EAA-S041

**Analytical adversarial test.** Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Known failure modes.** Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.; Introduced cost or harm: Prediction/evaluation expense, rigidity and possible preservation of harmful objectives.

**Contrary evidence.** **Statement.** Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.

**Source ids.** EAA-S053; EAA-S025; EAA-S041

**Is general refutation.** False

**Limit.** A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Open questions.** By which pre-existing criterion is the change judged, and is preserving that criterion itself justified?; Ideal rationality and accurate anticipation are strong; practical approximation and target validity remain unproved for open-ended deployed agents.

**Disposition rationale.** **Judgement.** Retain a narrow formal guard against successor self-certification, not general warrant for autonomous self-revision.

**Condition.** Explicit policy or utility self-modification under a model capable of representing its future effects.

**Limit.** Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.

**Not an unconditional obligation.** True
